# Perfect Kernel — progress ledger (living)

Protocol and status legend: [README.md](README.md). Newest sweep first.
Per-document artifacts live under `~/data/perfect_kernel/<bundle>/<name>/`
(out of repo); the sweep tally file is `~/data/perfect_kernel/sweep_verdicts.tsv`.

## Oracle pass (same-TL ground truth)

2026-08-31, TL2025, `tools/perfect_kernel/oracle.sh` (pdflatex, lualatex
fallback/detection, 90s): **1548 / 2374 oracle-clean** (1248 pdflatex + 300
lualatex), 801 DOCUMENT-STALE (the shipped .tex no longer compiles on this
TL — e.g. a4wide.tex vs siunitx v3), 25 timeout. **The S1 bar applies to the
1548 oracle-clean docs only.**

## S2 schema-validation baseline (sweep-5 XMLs)

2026-08-31, `tools/perfect_kernel/validate.sh` (jing, urn-resolved schema):
**1,409 / 2,329 produced core XMLs are RelaxNG-valid (60.5%)**; 10,899
validation error lines across the remainder (`validate_verdicts.tsv`).

**Sweep 27 re-measure (2026-09-01):** all completed docs **1,587 / 2,331
valid (68.1%, +7.6 pp)**; S0∧S1 slice **867 / 1,031 (84.1%)**. Top
signatures (whole set): `class` not NMTOKENs 2,157; `svg:foreignObject`
missing width/height 1,975; `xml:id` with colons 1,179; `stroke-linecap`
value 1,105; `text`/text inside `listing` (expects `listingline`) 375+116;
`block` misplaced 273; `tags` in inline context 221; `align` value 180;
`XMApp` outside Math 147. Worst docs: csvsimple-legacy 13,947,
chemplants-doc 3,659, optikz 2,024, xassoccnt 1,952, codeanatomy 1,825.
The first four signatures are mechanical attribute-shape fixes (≈63% of
the mass) — queued as lane S2a.

## S3 content-recall baseline (sweep-27 S0∧S1 slice)

2026-09-01, `tools/perfect_kernel/s3_audit.sh` (pdftotext word recall) over
the 1,031 S0∧S1 oracle-clean docs: **median 98.4%**; ≥95%: 763 (74.0%),
90-95%: 80, 80-90%: 59, 60-80%: 34, <60%: 95 (9.2%). No document has XML
> 2× the PDF word count (no duplication). 25 docs at **0.0%**: 10 have empty
PDFs (bclogo/syracuse, blochsphere, cartonaugh, comment/t1test, incgraph ×3,
babybeamer10, matrix-skeleton, pagelayout-guides — not bugs); 15 have
content the XML lacks entirely: knowledge (1,694 words), europecv (1,204),
figchild (925), ffslides-doc (871), cistercian-doc (240),
circularglyphs-doc (228), randintlist-l3-doc (172), frontespizio ×6,
fillwith, pgfPT.colorSchemes.info — queued as lane S3a (zero errors with
zero content is the worst kind of silent failure).

Steps 2-3 (retire the retractions; convert `Replace` bindings whose internals raw code reaches for into `Overlay`) follow. **Also in 56l (perf lane, pTeX family)**: **an unbalanced expansion is FATAL** (`definition/expandable.rs::Expandable::new`; Perl Core/Definition/Expandable.pm:35) — jarticle.cls:94-97's `\ds@tate` (an ISO-2022-JP byte pair whose `%` eats `\message`'s closing brace) was stored as-is and the run proceeded into japanese-otf's kanji scanners (platexcheat sample/platexsheet 253-259 s → ~2 s; RUST-ONLY, Perl aborts in 1.3 s); **`ajmacros.sty` bails with an explicit Fatal** (`ajmacros_sty.rs`; its ISO-2022-JP-named recursive kanji scanners, ajmacros.sty:246-271, spin `\advance\@tempcnta` in an APERIODIC loop — `\number\@tempcnta` changes every turn — that the cycle guard cannot see and only the 4 G token limit stops after ~250 s; platexsheet-jsclasses, sample-jsclasses, wtref-ja, jpneduenumerate; SHARED, Perl grinds too; an out-of-scope bail per DIFFICULT_CASES §D9, not a kanji emulation; ~1,500 s of sweep time removed). Settled dead-ends: `--token-limit` is re-raised by `scale_token_limit_to_source` (only `LATEXML_TOKEN_LIMIT` is honoured); lowering `DEFAULT_TOKEN_LIMIT` is wrong (source3 ~2 G, circularglyphs ~2.5 G legitimately). **Also in 56l (sweep-42 regressions, both from 56j)**: **raw `#`-bearing `\AtBeginDocument` chunks fire BEFORE the L3 hook** (`sect02.rs`, own store `@at@begin@document@rawparam`) — lthooks is FIFO within a label, and the 56j private-store carve-out ran alphabeta.sty:103-699's chunk after hep-math-font.sty:150-202's, flipping `\delta` into a self-loop (hep-paper-documentation PushbackLimit fatal; RUST-ONLY); still an interim under the K3 correctness item (the two-queue design cannot be FIFO in general). **tcb listing bodies keep POSITIONAL `#n` numbering** (`tcolorbox_sty.rs::renumber_param_tokens`): an absorbed specifier's `#n` becomes its default text (`D(){teal}` → `teal`), mapped slots are renumbered — `\NewTCBListing{egcite}{D(){ok} o m !o}{colframe=#1,…}` (oxyear-doc.tex:216) had fed the citation text to `colframe` (biblatex-oxref ×2 error growth; RUST-ONLY). **Fatals raised inside a class or binding load propagate** (`content.rs::load_class` / the binding-load catch): they fell to OmniBus / an `Error:unexpected` line, hiding the Fatal and continuing. **`DEFAULT_TOKEN_LIMIT` 4e9 → 20e9** (`gullet.rs`): at the measured 65 M tok/s ceiling 4e9 fired at 61 s on lie-hasse's legitimate E8 poset (completes at ~8e9 in 124 s with a 304 KB picture; pdflatex 83 s) and mislabelled it an infinite loop with a 39-byte XML (raise-not-reduce; the wall clock, cycle guards and RSS cap still bound true loops). **Perf verdicts recorded (agents, no code)**: tikz-network = datatool's raw expl3 per-cell `\DTLforeachkeyinrow`/`\DTLifeq` machinery (~1-2.7 s/row; streaming never activates because `Digested::estimate_bytes` caps at `EB_BUDGET=256`) → 4.8 GB MemoryBudget fatal with a 0-byte XML (Perl retains 4× more); the slow-with-output group is digestion-bound and linear (no O(n²) anywhere;
